Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` records a movement of cash between two accounts as\ntwo rows in category `TRANSFER`. The outgoing row carries `-amount` on `<from>` and the incoming row\ncarries `+amount` on `<to>`. Both rows are written together or neither is written.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The amount must be positive. The two accounts must differ. Either violation exits 3 and changes nothing.\n- A transfer that would leave either account balance below zero exits 3 and changes nothing.\n- On success the two rows take ids N and N+1, where N is one more than the largest id present.\n- On success the output header is `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`, followed by the outgoing row and then the incoming row.\n\n## Programmatic ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transfer.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transfer\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261021 V1 |\n| Description | Move cash between two accounts Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` records a movement of cash between two accounts as\ntwo rows in category `TRANSFER`. The outgoing row carries `-amount` on `<from>` and the incoming row\ncarries `+amount` on `<to>`. Both rows are written together or neither is written.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The amount must be positive. The two accounts must differ. Either violation exits 3 and changes nothing.\n- A transfer that would leave either account balance below zero exits 3 and changes nothing.\n- On success the two rows take ids N and N+1, where N is one more than the largest id present.\n- On success the output header is `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`, followed by the outgoing row and then the incoming row.\n\n## Programmatic
